Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the monte_carlo space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 756 and the convergence rate is O(485/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on monte_carlo structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 756. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 485. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the monte_carlo object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 97. The moduli space has dimension at most 231 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to monte_carlo theory, we decompose the problem into 97 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 231. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
